# Agents · I · part 11

1,177 entries, issue-raid-commander to issue-worker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **issue-raid-commander**: Analyzes agent:ready issues for merge conflicts and outputs a sprint plan. Read-only assessment — does NOT spawn agents, write code, or pick up issues. Use before spawning multiple issue-slayer agents in parallel. ([ugai/agent-guild-tavern](https://github.com/ugai/agent-guild-tavern/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild-tavern/agents/issue-raid-commander.md))
- **issue-ranger**: Scouts the codebase for bugs, improvement opportunities, and missing features, then posts well-scoped GitHub Issues for Issue Slayers to pick up. Discovery only — does NOT implement fixes or open PRs. Works standalone or as a team member. ([ugai/agent-guild-tavern](https://github.com/ugai/agent-guild-tavern/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild-tavern/agents/issue-ranger.md))
- **issue-reader**: Entry point for issue-driven development. Reads a GitHub issue via the github MCP server, extracts requirements, scope, acceptance criteria and a suggested branch name, then offers handoff to @feature-planner (formal spec in /plans/) or @quick-executor (autonomous ad-hoc execution). Read-only — never edits code or commits. ([lksnext-ai-lab/ai-core-tools](https://github.com/lksnext-ai-lab/ai-core-tools/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/issue-reader.agent.md) · ★26)
- **issue-reader**: Low-cost read-only analyst for GitHub issues, contracts, code, tests, Git state, and adversarial review. Use for the evidence-gathering and independent-review passes of the /issue workflow, or any time a bounded read-only investigation would otherwise burn a premium model. Never use it to write, edit, or publish anything.\n\nExamples:\n- &lt;example&gt;\n Context: Starting /issue 23 and needing the issue, parent epic, and acceptance criteria resolved.\n user: "/issue 23"\n assistant: "I'll dispatch the issue-reader agent to resolve issue 23, its parent, labels, dependencies, and acceptance criteria… ([sarveshcore/claude-skills](https://github.com/sarveshcore/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-reader.md) · ★1)

- **issue-refiner**: Issue Refiner — Analista de Issues GitLab. Agente especializado en obtener, analizar y mejorar issues de GitLab. Lee el título, descripción, comentarios y recursos de la issue, revisa el código fuente relevante del proyecto, y produce una descripción mejorada, estructurada y determinista. Si la tarea es vaga, genera preguntas concretas que deben resolverse antes de iniciar el desarrollo. Actualiza automáticamente la descripción de la issue en GitLab conservando la original. Toda la salida es en español. Invocar manualmente cuando se necesite refinar una issue.
